46a5(tog gzungs): dbyangs 'byin to//la la ni kha dog sna tshogs kyis bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logasu thal mo sbyar te bcom ldan 'das la ltar stod do//bdud dang bdud kyi 'khor la la ni bcom ldan
46a6(tog gzungs): 'das phyogs ga la ba der bzhugs pa de logasu mngon par phyogs te/bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir rin po che sna tshogs thogaso//la la ni srang dang khang pa dang/khyim dang 
46a7(tog gzungs): skar khung dang/rta babs dang/bsil khang dang/lam gyi gzhi mdo dang/sum mdo dang/khang pa brtsegs pa dang/sgo khang dang/shing dang/gzhal med khang rnams na 'dug cing thal

46b6(tog gzungs): na klog snang yang mi dmigs pa yin no//'di lta ste/dper na/grib ma snang yang mi dmigs pa de bzhin du/pad mo de yang snang mod kyi mi dmigaso//gang gi tshe bdud sdig can gyis

47a3(tog gzungs): pa de'i tshe/yang 'gul pa bcings pa lngas bcings pa dag gis shes te/gang gi tshe gang du yang 'gro ma nus pa de'i tshe/bcom ldan 'das la mgon dang skyabasu sems bskyed nas

47b7(tog gzungs): gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ma gshegs sh-ig/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi yul bsam gyis mi khyab//bdud kyi yul bsam gyis mi khya ba//klu'i yul bas
